### 👤 User (Step 1 - 2026-10-25T17:05:31Z)

/plan Add to the gp-coding-plugin a first set of subagents and pre prompt hooks

Design rules:
- worker subagents must be implemented in python using google-antigravity SDK (LocalAgentConfig + antigravity agent). assign a model that is matching the complexity of the task. use google-antigravity integration (ask_question, artifact, task, ...)
- workflow subagents must be implemented in python using google-adk SDK (Workflow) when Graph workflow are used, using google-antigravity SDK.
- when the subagent can be mentioned, it means that they must be visible to be directly called by the user. 
- agent should use formatted output (JSON) with rigid schema. write Markdown converter to present the output to the user.

## 1. coding-orchestrator subagent (can be mentioned as @coding)

It will propose the following workflow: coding-clarify → coding-specify → coding-plan → coding-develop → coding → review → coding-merge.

- **coding-clarify**: a dedicated worker subagent that will grill the user to clarify the requirement. Once all question are answered, it will pass the result to the **coding-specify** subagent asking to write the specification for the requirements. Based the instruction of the agent on "mattpocock" `/grill-me` skill.
- **coding-specify**: a dedicated worker subagent that will consolidate the requirement into a specification (artifact 'specification.md'). The user can:
  - approve the artifact: move to the next step, coding-
  - reject the artifact: ask the user to add more information, pass it to the coding-clarify to resume the workflow
  - prompt something while the artifact is not yet approved or rejected: pass the prompt to the coding-clarify to resume the workflow
- **coding-plan**: a dedicated worker subagent that will convert the specification into a plan (artifact 'plan.md'). It will break instructions per repository (if multiple repository), determine branch name (`feature/`, `task/` or `bugfix/`). The user can:
  - approve the artifact: move to the next step, coding-develop
  - reject the artifact: ask the user to add more information, pass it to the coding-clarify to resume the workflow
  - prompt something while the artifact is not yet approved or rejected: pass the prompt to the coding-clarify to resume the workflow
- **coding-develop**: a dedicated worker subagent to perform the development tasks (write code, unit test, check code quality, update repo documentation, update CI/CD, .... It can only work in a single repository (in case of multi repository project, it will set the workdir to the repository). Multiple can be fired in parallel is the change involves multiple repository. It will report the result to the coding-review subagent.
- **coding-review**: a dedicated worker subagent which will wait for all **coding-develop** subagent ot complete the task. it will review each agent report and validate that the changes are consistent accross all repos, that is matches the original request and that it only covers the request. if any discrepency discovered, it will as the **coding-plan** subagent to plan necessary modifications (it will be a new plan) and the workflow will resume here. If all is good, it will ask the user if he wants to merge the changes
- **coding-merge**: a dedicated worker subagent which will either merge back the development branch to master (if no remote repository set) or submit a pull-request (if a remote repository is set). if a pull request is created, it will wait for the approval. 

All the worker subagent will work in background, appearing a background subagent. subagents awaiting user input will wait in background, the user can message them to awake them back and resume (**coding-specify** will wait for the specification approval, **coding-plan** will wait for the plan approval, **coding-review** will wait for the user merge approval).

Only one coding workflow at the time. When the workflow complete, another can start. Keep in state if the workflow is started, which step it is. Because subagent are in background, make sure that approving or rejecting the artifact will reach the subagent awaiting the response.

Since multiple **coding-develop** can run in parallel (but only one per repo), add the repo name to their name for easy identification.

## 2. coding-monitoring agent (can be mentioned as @monitoring)

This is standalone worker subagent which role will consist of monitoring application logs, serial console, etc. (based on what the user asks). it will look for error and should one be found, it will ask what the user wants to do. Multiple **coding-monitoring** subagent can be running in parallel (for example on watching serial console with the other monitoring application logs). Make sure each subagent has an additional identifier so it is easy to know what one is doing.

## 3. Prompt triage hook

A hook is going to be used to analyze prompt: triage them to the proper agent. The hook will use Laya Router to (in that specific order):
1. First, evaluate if the prompt is related to the scope of the session (if the session is just started, no need to analyze). If confidence is >= 0.8, move on the next phase, otherwise ask the user if he wants to continue (i.e. broaden the scope of the session) or stop here (in which case it stops here).
2. Check if a coding workflow is started and one or more subagent are waiting user input:,
   - if true, triage the prompt if it corresponds to a subagent waiting user input. If one is identified with a confidence >= 0.8, message the subagent so it can handle the user prompt
3. Third, triage the prompt evaluating if it is a question, coding request or monitoring request. if it can identify one with a confidence >= 0.8, it will automatically continue, Otherwise it will ask the user to confirm. Here is what it should be based on the determination (auto or selected by the user)
   - a question: pass it to the main agent with the following alteration: "Do not make any changes".
   - a coding request: alter the prompt to request the main agent to invoke the **coding-orchestrator** subagent. if a coding workflow is already started, replace it by indicating if it concerns a subagent waiting user input.
   - a monitoring request: alter the prompt to request the main agent to invoke the **coding-monitoring** subagent.

## 4. Main agent awareness

The main agent should be aware of the availability of the subagent and be able to invoke them using "invoke_subagent". It should be possible to message a subagent awaiting user input (`@coding-specification It should also be possible to perform this action if...`).

> Initial focus is on understanding the mechanisms by which the main agent interacts with and utilizes subagents. The user request explicitly defines the implementation using a specific SDK, implying a defined registration or invocation process. Careful examination of the request details is necessary to determine the exact method of subagent management within this framework.
> 
> 
> The core architecture necessitates a hierarchical workflow with `@coding` orchestrating subagents for code generation. The "mattpocock" /grill-me approach will drive user interaction for requirement clarification via `@coding-clarify`. The `@coding-specify` subagent then synthesizes a specification, which then feeds the `@coding-plan` subagent to generate the execution roadmap.
> 
> 
> The agent's decision regarding artifact approval dictates the workflow's progression. Artifact approval triggers the `@coding-develop` subagent, initiating the development phase. Rejection, or the need for clarification, routes the process through `@coding-clarify` to seek further information. The `@coding-develop` subagent operates on individual repositories, handling development tasks and reporting to `@coding-review`.
> 
> The `@coding-review` subagent ensures consistency across repositories and the original request, passing any necessary modifications back to planning. Once validated, it prompts user interaction for merge approval via the `@coding-merge` subagent. The `@coding-merge` subagent handles either direct merging or pull request submission based on the remote repository configuration.
> 
> 
> The system will manage subagents by placing them in a background state when awaiting input, resuming them upon receiving messages.  A single coding workflow will be active at any given time, progressing through specific steps (specify, plan, develop, review). Multiple `coding-develop` agents will run concurrently, distinguished by repository name. Each monitoring agent will have unique identifier.
> 
> 
> Initial assessment involves establishing confidence thresholds for decision-making and incorporating user input to guide the session's scope. A key step involves identifying if a coding workflow has commenced and if any subagents are waiting for user input. The prompt must be triaged to determine if it is a question, coding request, or monitoring request.
> 
> 
> The main agent leverages `rules/AGENTS.md` to discover and define subagents. This file outlines subagent availability and invocation methods using specific syntax. A pre-invocation hook within `hooks.json` provides a mechanism for session management and workflow state checking before the core model execution.

> 
> The code review subagent assesses the completed development work, ensuring consistency across repositories and adherence to the initial request. > All worker subagents operate in the background. The monitoring subagent actively observes application logs for errors, prompting user action when issues arise.

> 
> 
> The prompt triage system uses a router to determine the intent of user requests, with a confidence threshold for scope and intent.   "Content": "\"\"\"Workflow state management for gp-coding-plugin.\n\nMaintains concurrency locking (single active workflow at a time),\nstep progression, waiting subagents, and persistence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom enum import Enum\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n\nclass WorkflowConflictError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when an attempt is made to start a coding workflow while another is active.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass WorkflowStatus(str, Enum):\n    IDLE = \"IDLE\"\n    CLARIFYING = \"CLARIFYING\"\n    SPECIFYING = \"SPECIFYING\"\n    AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL = \"AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL\"\n    PLANNING = \"PLANNING\"\n    AWAITING_PLAN_APPROVAL = \"AWAITING_PLAN_APPROVAL\"\n    DEVELOPING = \"DEVELOPING\"\n    REVIEWING = \"REVIEWING\"\n    AWAITING_MERGE_APPROVAL = \"AWAITING_MERGE_APPROVAL\"\n    MERGING = \"MERGING\"\n    COMPLETED = \"COMPLETED\"\n    FAILED = \"FAILED\"\n\n    def is_active(self) -> bool:\n        return self not in (WorkflowStatus.IDLE, WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED, WorkflowStatus.FAILED)\n\n    def is_waiting_user_input(self) -> bool:\n        return self in (\n            WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL,\n            WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_PLAN_APPROVAL,\n            WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_MERGE_APPROVAL,\n        )\n\n\nclass WorkflowState(BaseModel):\n    workflow_id: str = Field(default=\"\", description=\"Unique identifier for the current workflow run\")\n    status: WorkflowStatus = Field(default=WorkflowStatus.IDLE, description=\"Current status of the workflow\")\n    current_step: str = Field(default=\"\", description=\"Identifier of the active subagent or step\")\n    waiting_agent: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, description=\"Name of the subagent waiting for user input\")\n    waiting_conversation_id: Optional[str] = Field(\n        default=None, description=\"Conversation ID of the subagent waiting for message delivery\"\n    )\n    repositories: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Target repository names or relative paths\")\n    active_develop_agents: dict[str, str] = Field(\n        default_factory=dict, description=\"Mapping of repo_name -> develop subagent name or conversationId\"\n    )\n    artifacts: dict[str, str] = Field(\n        default_factory=dict, description=\"Artifact references (e.g. {'specification': path, 'plan': path})\"\n    )\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description=\"Additional context or state payloads\")\n\n    @classmethod\n    def get_default_path(cls, artifact_dir: Optional[str] = None, conversation_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Resolve standard state file path.\"\"\"\n        if artifact_dir:\n            p = Path(artifact_dir) / \"coding_workflow_state.json\"\n            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n            return p\n        if conversation_id:\n            home = Path.home()\n            p = home / \".gemini\" / \"antigravity-cli\" / \"brain\" / conversation_id / \"coding_workflow_state.json\"\n            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n            return p\n        # Fallback to local .agents directory\n        p = Path(\".agents\") / \"coding_workflow_state.json\"\n        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        return p\n\n    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, path: Optional[Path] = None, artifact_dir: Optional[str] = None, conversation_id: Optional[str] = None) -> WorkflowState:\n        target_path = path or cls.get_default_path(artifact_dir, conversation_id)\n        if not target_path.exists():\n            return cls()\n        try:\n            with open(target_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                data = json.load(f)\n            return cls.model_validate(data)\n        except Exception:\n            return cls()\n\n    def save(self, path: Optional[Path] = None, artifact_dir: Optional[str] = None, conversation_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Path:\n        target_path = path or self.get_default_path(artifact_dir, conversation_id)\n        target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        with open(target_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            f.write(self.model_dump_json(indent=2))\n        return target_path\n\n    def start_workflow(self, workflow_id: str, repositories: Optional[list[str]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize a new workflow. Enforces single active workflow constraint.\"\"\"\n        if self.status.is_active():\n            raise WorkflowConflictError(\n                f\"Cannot start workflow '{workflow_id}': another workflow '{self.workflow_id}' \"\n                f\"is currently active at step '{self.current_step}' (status: {self.status.value}).\"\n            )\n        self.workflow_id = workflow_id\n        self.status = WorkflowStatus.CLARIFYING\n        self.current_step = \"coding-clarify\"\n        self.waiting_agent = None\n        self.waiting_conversation_id = None\n        self.repositories = repositories or []\n        self.active_develop_agents = {}\n        self.artifacts = {}\n        self.metadata = {}\n\n    def transition_to(\n        self,\n        status: WorkflowStatus,\n        step: str,\n        waiting_agent: Optional[str] = None,\n        waiting_conversation_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Transition workflow to the next step and optionally mark a waiting subagent.\"\"\"\n        self.status = status\n        self.current_step = step\n        self.waiting_agent = waiting_agent\n        if waiting_conversation_id is not None:\n            self.waiting_conversation_id = waiting_conversation_id\n\n    def complete_workflow(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark workflow as successfully completed.\"\"\"\n        self.status = WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED\n        self.current_step = \"completed\"\n        self.waiting_agent = None\n        self.waiting_conversation_id = None\n\n    def fail_workflow(self, reason: str = \"\") -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark workflow as failed.\"\"\"\n        self.status = WorkflowStatus.FAILED\n        self.current_step = \"failed\"\n        self.waiting_agent = None\n        self.waiting_conversation_id = None\n        self.metadata[\"failure_reason\"] = reason\n",

}

description=\"Recommended answer prefixed with (Recommended)\")\n    reasoning: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, description=\"Rationale for the recommendation\")\n\n\nclass ClarificationSummary(BaseModel):\n    requirement_title: str = Field(description=\"Summary title of the requirement being clarified\")\n    answered_questions: dict[str, str] = Field(\n        default_factory=dict, description=\"Mapping of question -> user answer\"\n    )\n    confirmed_scope: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Explicitly in-scope items\")\n    out_of_scope: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Explicitly out-of-scope items\")\n    unresolved_questions: list[str] = Field(\n        default_factory=list, description=\"Questions still awaiting clarification\"\n    )\n    ready_for_spec: bool = Field(\n        default=False, description=\"True when all ambiguities are resolved and ready for specification\"\n    )\n\n\ndef clarification_to_markdown(summary: ClarificationSummary) -> str:\n    lines = [\n        f\"## Requirement Clarification: {summary.requirement_title}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"### Confirmed Scope\",\n    ]\n    if summary.confirmed_scope:\n        lines.extend([f\"- {item}\" for item in summary.confirmed_scope])\n    else:\n        lines.append(\"- *(None specified)*\")\n\n    lines.extend([\"\", \"### Out of Scope\"])\n    if summary.out_of_scope:\n        lines.extend([f\"- {item}\" for item in summary.out_of_scope])\n    else:\n        lines.append(\"- *(None specified)*\")\n\n    lines.extend([\"\", \"### Resolved Questions & Answers\"])\n    if summary.answered_questions:\n        for q, a in summary.answered_questions.items():\n Pending Questions\"])\n        for q in summary.unresolved_questions:\n            lines.append(f\"- [ ] {q}\")\n\n    lines.extend([\"\", f\"**Ready for Specification:** {'✅ Yes' if ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass SpecificationDocument(BaseModel):\n    title: str = Field(description=\"Feature specification title\")\n    summary: str = Field(description=\"Executive summary of the feature\")\n    background: str = Field(description=\"Problem statement and motivation\")\n    functional_requirements: list[str] = Field(description=\"List of functional requirements\")\n    non_functional_requirements: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Quality and performance criteria\")\n    affected_repositories: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"List of repositories involved\")\n    edge_cases: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Edge cases and mitigations\")\n    acceptance_criteria: list[str] = Field(description=\"Verifiable acceptance criteria\")\n    approved: Optional[bool] = Field(default=None, description=\"User approval status (None=pending, True=approved, False=rejected)\")\n\n\ndef specification_to_markdown(spec: SpecificationDocument) -> str:\n    lines = [\n        f\"# Specification: {spec.title}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Approval Status:** {'✅ Approved' if spec.approved is True else ('❌ Rejected' if spec.approved is False else '⏳ Pending Approval')}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"## Summary\",\n        spec.summary,\n        \"\",\n        \"## Background & Motivation\",\n        spec.background,\n        \"\",\n        \"## Functional Requirements\",\n    ]\n    lines.extend([f\"- {req}\" for req in spec.functional_requirements])\n\n    if spec.non_functional_requirements:\n        lines.extend([\"\", \"## Non-Functional Requirements\"])\n        lines.extend([f\"- {req}\" for req in lines.extend([f\"- {ec}\" for ec in spec.edge_cases])\n\n    lines.extend([\"\", \"## Acceptance Criteria\"])\n    lines.extend([f\"- [ ] {ac}\" for ac in spec.acceptance_criteria])\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 3. coding-plan\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass PlanStep(BaseModel):\n    step_number: int = Field(description=\"Sequential step index\")\n    repository: str = task/, or bugfix/ convention\")\n    files_to_create: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Files to be created\")\n    files_to_modify: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Files to be edited\")\n    verification_commands: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Commands to verify changes\")\n\n\nclass PlanDocument(BaseModel):\n    title: str = Field(description=\"Implementation plan title\")\n    summary: str = Field(description=\"Plan summary and architecture overview\")\n    branch_strategy: dict[str, str] = Field(\n        default_factory=dict, description=\"Mapping of repo_name -> branch name (feature/*, task/*, bugfix/*)\"\n    )\n    steps: list[PlanStep] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Ordered implementation steps per repository\")\n    risks_and_mitigations: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"Identified risks and mitigations\")\n    approved: Optional[bool] = Field(default=None, description=\"User approval status (None=pending, True=approved, False=rejected)\")\n\n\ndef plan_to_markdown(plan: PlanDocument) -> str:\n    lines = [\n        f\"# Implementation Plan: {plan.title}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Approval Status:** {'✅ Approved' if plan.approved is True else ('❌ Rejected' if plan.approved is False else '⏳ Pending Steps\"])\n    for step in plan.steps:\n        lines.extend([\n            f\"### Step {step.step_number}: {step.description} (`{step.repository}`)\",\n            f\"- **Branch:** **Verify:** `{'; '.join(step.verification_commands)}`\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    if plan.risks_and_mitigations:\n        lines.extend([\"## Risks & Mitigations\"])\n        lines.extend([f\"- {rm}\" for rm in plan.risks_and_mitigations])\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 4. coding-develop\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass DevelopReport(BaseModel):\n    repository: str = Field(description=\"Target repository name\")\n    branch: str = Field(description=\"Active branch name\")\n    files_created: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description=\"List of created files\")\n    files_modified: list[str] =   f\"- **Branch:** `{report.branch}`\",\n        f\"- **Tests:** {'✅ Passed' if report.tests_passed else '❌ Failed'}\",\n        f\"- **Linting:** {'✅ Clean' if report.lint_passed else '❌ Issues      \"#### Changes Applied\",\n    ]\n    if report.files_created:\n        lines.append(f\"- **Created:** {', '.join(f'`{f}`' for f in report.files_created)}\")\n    if report.files_modified:\n > [!quote]- 💭 Reasoning Trace
> The system successfully processed the query, predicting the intent as a question with high confidence. Proceeding to analyze the triage stages based on the user's specific request for prompt analysis.
> 
> 
> First, the session scope is evaluated. If the confidence of relevance is below a threshold, the user will be asked to confirm if the prompt is within the scope. Then, if a coding workflow is active, the prompt will be triaged to determine if it's targeted towards a waiting subagent, if so it will be directly routed to that subagent. Finally the prompt will be classified as a question, coding request, or monitoring request.
> 
> 
> Analyzing the prompt triage process reveals a structured approach to managing user requests. Session initiation or first invocation triggers an evaluation of scope. Subsequent invocations employ a confidence-based scope check, alerting the user if the prompt appears outside the established session's bounds. A coding workflow check will direct prompts to a subagent if one is waiting for input.
> 
> 
> Checking if the prompt addresses a waiting subagent, such as for specification approval, begins with assessing relevance. Prompts are classified as "respond_to_waiting_agent" if they are approvals, rejections, comments, or refinements, otherwise "unrelated."  If relevance confidence is high, the prompt is injected with an instruction to directly message the designated subagent, resuming the active workflow step.
> 
> 
> A high confidence score triggers specific prompt alterations based on intent. Questions are passed directly with an instruction to avoid modifications. Coding requests, if part of an existing workflow, are routed to the relevant subagent. Otherwise, a request is made to initiate a coding workflow. Monitoring requests also trigger a direct call to the monitoring subagent.

TriageResult(BaseModel):\n    stage_reached: int = Field(description=\"Last evaluation stage reached (1, 2, or 3)\")\n    in_scope: bool = Field(default=True, description=\"Whether prompt is in payload\")\n\n\nclass LayaTriageEngine:\n    \"\"\"Wrapper around Laya Router with heuristic fallback.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, use_router: bool = True):\n        self._router = None\n        if use_router:\n            try:\n                from laya import Router\n                self._router = Router(preload=False)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.warning(f\"Could not initialize Laya Router, using fallback: {e}\")\n                self._router = None\n\n    def _predict_with_laya(self, text: str, questions: dict[str, Any]) -> conf\n\n        # Deterministic fallback\n        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()\n        off_topic_words = [\"weather\", \"recipe\", \"poem\", \"sports\", \"dinner\", \"vacation\"]\n        if any(w   return True, 0.90\n        return False, 0.50\n\n    def evaluate_intent(self, prompt: str) -> tuple[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Stage 3: Triage prompt into question, coding, or monitoring.\"\"\"\n        questions = {\n            \"intent\": {\n                \"type\": \"choice\",\n                \"instructions\": \"Classify the primary intent into: question, coding, or monitoring.\",\n           \"coding\": \"Asking to create, modify, implement, refactor, test, or build code, or running a development workflow.\",\n                    \"monitoring\": \"Asking to monitor, watch, prompt.lower()\n        if any(w in prompt_lower for w in [\"monitor\", \"watch logs\", \"tail -f\", \"serial console\", \"log stream\", \"@monitoring\"]):\n            return \"monitoring\", 0.92\n return \"question\", 0.85\n\n    def triage(\n        self,\n        prompt: str,\n        invocation_num: int = 1,\n        state: Optional[WorkflowState] = None,\n    ) -> TriageResult:\n \"systemMessage\": (\n                        \"Prompt Triage Alert: The prompt appears out of the scope of this session \"\n                        f\"(confidence: {scope_conf:.2f}). Ask the user whether they want to \"\n                        \"broaden the scope of the session or stop here.\"\n                    )\n                }\n            })\n            return TriageResult(\n                stage_reached=1,\n                in_scope=in_scope,\n                scope_confidence=scope_conf,\n                inject_steps=inject_steps,\n            )\n\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Stage 2: Check Active Workflow & Waiting Subagent\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------\n        if state.status.is_waiting_user_input() and state.waiting_agent:\n            matches, match_conf = self.evaluate_waiting_agent_match(prompt, state.waiting_agent)\n            if matches and match_conf >= 0.80:\n                conv_target = state.waiting_conversation_id or state.waiting_agent\n                inject_steps.append({\n                    \"systemMessage\": {\n                        \"systemMessage\": (\n                            f\"Prompt Triage: Routed to waiting subagent '{state.waiting_agent}' \"\n                            f\"(Target: {conv_target}). Deliver the prompt directly to '{state.waiting_agent}' \"\n                            f\"using send_message to resume workflow step '{state.current_step}'.\"\n                        )\n                    }\n                })\n                return TriageResult(\n                    stage_reached=2,\n                    in_scope=True,\n                    scope_confidence=scope_conf,\n                    routed_to_waiting_agent=state.waiting_agent,\n                    inject_steps=inject_steps,\n                )\n\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Stage 3: Intent Classification (Question, Coding, Monitoring)\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------\n        intent, intent_conf = self.evaluate_intent(prompt)\n\n        if intent_conf < 0.80:\n            # Please invoke the coding-monitoring subagent (@monitoring) \"\n                        \"to track the requested log stream or console output.\"\n                    )\n                }\n            })\n\n        return TriageResult(\n            stage_reached=3,\n            in_scope=True,\n            scope_confidence=scope_conf,\n            intent=intent,\n            intent_confidence=intent_conf,\n            inject_steps=inject_steps,\n        )\n",

{
  "Content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"PreInvocation Hook: Laya Router Prompt Triage.\n\nReceives Antigravity hook payload on stdin and emits injectSteps on stdout.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Ensure src/ is importable\nPLUGIN_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nSRC_DIR = PLUGIN_ROOT / \"src\"\nif str(SRC_DIR) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC_DIR))\n\ntry:\n    from gp_coding.state import WorkflowState\n    from gp_coding.triage import LayaTriageEngine\nexcept Exception as e:\n    # Graceful fallback if imports fail\n    json.dump({\"injectSteps\": []}, sys.stdout)\n    sys.exit(0)\n\n\ndef main():\n    try:\n        raw_input = sys.stdin.read()\n        if not raw_input.strip():\n            json.dump({\"injectSteps\": []}, sys.stdout)\n            return\n\n        payload = json.loads(raw_input)\n        prompt = payload.get(\"lastUserInput\", \"\")\n        invocation_num = payload.get(\"invocationNum\", 1)\n        conv_id = payload.get(\"conversationId\")\n        artifact_dir = payload.get(\"artifactDirectoryPath\")\n\n        if not prompt:\n            json.dump({\"injectSteps\": []}, sys.stdout)\n            return\n\n        state = WorkflowState.load(artifact_dir=artifact_dir, conversation_id=conv_id)\n        engine = LayaTriageEngine(use_router=True)\n        result = engine.triage(prompt=prompt, invocation_num=invocation_num, state=state)\n\n        json.dump({\"injectSteps\": result.inject_steps}, sys.stdout)\n    except Exception:\n        # Hooks must never crash the Antigravity agent process\n        json.dump({\"injectSteps\": []}, sys.stdout)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py"
}

### 🤖 Agent (Step 133 - 2026-10-25T17:26:54Z)

> [!quote]- 💭 Reasoning Trace
> Identifying necessary worker subagents for the code generation process. The first is a clarification agent (`clarify.py`) to gather missing requirements from the user. This agent will employ a question-asking strategy and use a file-reading capability. The output will be validated against a predefined format and then passed to the specification subagent upon completion.
> 
> 
> A `specify.py` agent will synthesize requirements into a specification artifact, awaiting user approval. The `plan.py` agent will then convert the specification into a development plan, handling multi-repository scenarios and determining branch naming, also requiring user approval. Development tasks, including code generation and testing, are handled by `develop.py`.
> 
> 
> A review agent will ensure code consistency across repositories, validating changes against the original requirements. Discrepancies will trigger replanning, while successful reviews lead to a merge approval request.
> 
> A merge agent will handle branch merging or pull request submission, awaiting approval if a remote repository is configured, then emitting a merge report.
> 
> A monitoring agent will continuously watch for errors in application logs and serial consoles, prompting user intervention when necessary and can be run in parallel.
> 
> The orchestrator agent, powered by a specific SDK, drives the workflow: clarification, specification, planning, development, review, and merging.

  "Content": "\"\"\"coding-specify worker subagent.\n\nConsolidates clarified requirements into a formal specification artifact (specification.md).\nHandles user approval, rejection, and mid-review feedback loops.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\nfrom google.antigravity import Agent, CapabilitiesConfig, LocalAgentConfig\n\nfrom gp_coding.schemas import SpecificationDocument, specification_to_markdown\n\nSPECIFY_SYSTEM_PROMPT = \"\"\"You are coding-specify, a software specification architect subagent.\n\nYour role:\n1. Receive requirement clarifications from coding-clarify.\n2. Synthesize them into an exhaustive, unambiguous specification adhering to the SpecificationDocument schema.\n3. Write the specification artifact as 'specification.md' in the session artifact directory with RequestFeedback=true.\n4. Convert and present the specification to the user using the markdown converter.\n5. Await user feedback in the background:\n   - If the user APPROVES the artifact: signal readiness to proceed to coding-plan.\n   - If the user REJECTS the artifact: request additional details and route back to coding-clarify.\n   - If the user provides extra PROMPTS or FEEDBACK before approving/rejecting: route the feedback to coding-clarify to update requirements.\n\"\"\"\n\nSPECIFY_MODEL = \"gemini-3.8-flash\"\n\n\ndef create_specify_config(\n    workspace_path: Optional[str] = None,\n    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> LocalAgentConfig:\n    \"\"\"Create LocalAgentConfig for coding-specify worker subagent.\"\"\"\n    capabilities = CapabilitiesConfig(\n        enable_subagents=False,\n        enabled_tools=[\"write_file\", \"edit_file\", \"read_file\", \"ask_question\"],\n    )\n    return LocalAgentConfig(\n        system_instructions=SPECIFY_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n        capabilities=capabilities,\n        model=SPECIFY_MODEL,\n        response_schema=SpecificationDocument,\n        workspaces=[workspace_path] if workspace_path else None,\n        conversation_id=conversation_id,\n    )\n\n\ndef create_specify_agent(\n    workspace_path: Optional[str] = None,\n    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> Agent:\n    \"\"\"Instantiate coding-specify agent.\"\"\"\n    config = create_specify_config(workspace_path, conversation_id)\n    return Agent(config)\n",

}

  "Content": "\"\"\"coding-plan worker subagent.\n\nConverts approved specifications into a multi-repo implementation plan artifact (plan.md).\nAllocates tasks per repository, selects branch naming conventions (feature/*, task/*, bugfix/*),\nand gates execution on user approval.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\nfrom google.antigravity import Agent, CapabilitiesConfig, LocalAgentConfig\n\nfrom gp_coding.schemas import PlanDocument, plan_to_markdown\n\nPLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT = \"\"\"You are coding-plan, a principal software architect subagent.\n\nYour role:\n1. Ingest the approved specification from coding-specify.\n2. Break the implementation down per repository (identifying mono-repo vs multi-repo boundaries).\n3. Determine appropriate git branch names for each repository strictly following:\n   - feature/<name> for new capabilities\n   - task/<name> for refactorings, chores, or maintenance\n   - bugfix/<name> for bug corrections\n4. Structure atomic execution steps, files to create/modify, and concrete verification commands conforming to PlanDocument.\n5. Write the plan artifact as 'plan.md' in the session artifact directory with RequestFeedback=true.\n6. Await user feedback in the background:\n   - If the user APPROVES the artifact: transition the workflow to coding-develop.\n   - If the user REJECTS the artifact: solicit missing details and route back to coding-clarify.\n   - If the user sends a PROMPT or feedback: route back to coding-clarify to re-align requirements.\n\"\"\"\n\nPLAN_MODEL = \"gemini-3.8-pro\"\n\n\ndef create_plan_config(\n    workspace_path: Optional[str] = None,\n    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> LocalAgentConfig:\n    \"\"\"Create LocalAgentConfig for coding-plan worker subagent.\"\"\"\n    capabilities = CapabilitiesConfig(\n        enable_subagents=False,\n        enabled_tools=[\"write_file\", \"edit_file\", \"read_file\", \"run_command\", \"ask_question\"],\n    )\n    return LocalAgentConfig(\n        system_instructions=PLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n        capabilities=capabilities,\n        model=PLAN_MODEL,\n        response_schema=PlanDocument,\n        workspaces=[workspace_path] if workspace_path else None,\n        conversation_id=conversation_id,\n    )\n\n\ndef create_plan_agent(\n    workspace_path: Optional[str] = None,\n    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> Agent:\n    \"\"\"Instantiate coding-plan agent.\"\"\"\n    config = create_plan_config(workspace_path, conversation_id)\n    return Agent(config)\n",

{
  "Content": "# Coding Standards & Subagent Coordination — gp-coding-plugin\n\nThis rule set applies whenever `gp-coding-plugin` is active in an Antigravity session.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Core Engineering Principles\n- **Clarity over Cleverness**: Write readable, expressive code with self-explanatory naming and explicit typing.\n- **Fail Fast & Explicitly**: Validate boundaries, function arguments, and I/O operations early.\n- **Single Responsibility**: Functions and classes should have one well-defined responsibility.\n- **Preserve Comments & Docstrings**: Never strip existing comments or docstrings unless explicitly instructed.\n- **Clickable Links**: Always create clickable file and code symbol links using markdown with the `file://` scheme.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Subagent Architecture & Mentions\n\nThe plugin provides a coordinated suite of specialized subagents. When users mention these subagents (e.g. `@coding`, `@monitoring`), or when the prompt triage hook directs you to invoke them, you must use `invoke_subagent` and `send_message`.\n\n### 2.1 Mentionable Subagents\n\n| Mention Handle | Subagent Name | Role | SDK & Model |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| **`@coding`** | `coding-orchestrator` | ADK Workflow graph managing the 6-phase coding lifecycle | Google ADK 2.0 (`Workflow`) |\n| **`@monitoring`** | `coding-monitoring` | Telemetry & stream surveillance (logs, serial console) | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-flash-lite`) |\n| `@coding-clarify` | `coding-clarify` | Matt Pocock `/grill-me` requirement questionnaire | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-flash`) |\n| `@coding-specify` | `coding-specify` | Requirement synthesis & `specification.md` artifact | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-flash`) |\n| `@coding-plan` | `coding-plan` | Multi-repo architecture & `plan.md` artifact | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-pro`) |\n| `@coding-develop-<repo>`| `coding-develop` | Per-repo isolated development & test execution | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-pro`) |\n| `@coding-review` | `coding-review` | Cross-repo consistency & specification validation | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-pro`) |\n| `@coding-merge` | `coding-merge` | Git branch merge / Pull Request submission | `google-antigravity` (`gemini-3.8-flash-lite`) |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Coding Workflow Lifecycle\n\n```text\ncoding-clarify ──> coding-specify ──> coding-plan ──> coding-develop (parallel per repo)\n                          ▲               ▲                      │\n                          │ (reject/fb)   │ (reject/fb)          ▼\n                          └───────────────┴────────────── coding-review\n                                                                 │ (approved)\n                                                                 ▼\n                                                           coding-merge\n```\n\n### 3.1 Lifecycle Rules:\n1. **Single Workflow Concurrency**: Only ONE coding workflow can run at a time. The workflow state is tracked in `workflow_state.json`. Before starting a new workflow, ensure the active one is either `COMPLETED` or `FAILED`.\n2. **Background Execution & Resumption**: Worker subagents execute in the background. Subagents awaiting user decisions (`coding-specify` awaiting spec approval, `coding-plan` awaiting plan approval, `coding-review` awaiting merge approval) wait in background state.\n3. **Message Forwarding**: When the user approves or rejects an artifact, or supplies feedback addressed to an awaiting subagent (e.g. `@coding-specify Add support for gRPC`), route the message directly to that subagent's conversation ID using `send_message`.\n4. **Structured JSON Output**: Subagents return rigid structured JSON schemas defined in `gp_coding.schemas`. Always use the markdown presentation helpers to display results cleanly to the user.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Prompt Triage Hook Behavior\n\nThe plugin registers a `PreInvocation` hook (`hooks/prompt_triage.py`) driven by **Laya Router**:\n1. **Session Scope Guard**: Assesses whether prompts pertain to software engineering in this session. If confidence is $< 0.80$, the main agent will receive an instruction to ask the user to clarify scope.\n2. **Subagent Waiting Route**: Automatically identifies if the prompt responds to an awaiting subagent and injects routing guidance to wake up that subagent.\n3. **Intent Triage**:\n   - **Question**: Main agent is instructed: *\"Do not make any changes.\"*\n   - **Coding Request**: Prompts invocation of `@coding` orchestrator (or references the currently active workflow).\n   - **Monitoring Request**: Prompts invocation of `@monitoring` subagent.\n",

  "Content": "---\nname: coding-orchestrator\ndescription: >-\n  Orchestrate full-lifecycle software development tasks through the 6-stage\n  coordinated workflow: coding-clarify, coding-specify, coding-plan,\n  coding-develop, coding-review, and coding-merge.\n---\n\n# Coding Orchestrator (`@coding`)\n\nThe `coding-orchestrator` guides software requests through a rigorous, multi-agent development pipeline.\n\n## Workflow Pipeline\n\n1. **`coding-clarify`**:\n   - Conducts a Matt Pocock `/grill-me` style interview.\n   - Clarifies edge cases, non-goals, and dependencies one question at a time via `ask_question`.\n2. **`coding-specify`**:\n   - Synthesizes findings into `specification.md` (Artifact).\n   - Awaits user approval, rejection, or feedback in the background.\n3. **`coding-plan`**:\n   - Decomposes work per repository and determines branch names (`feature/*`, `task/*`, `bugfix/*`).\n   - Produces `plan.md` (Artifact) and awaits user approval.\n4. **`coding-develop`**:\n   - Fanned out in parallel per repository (`coding-develop-<repo>`).\n   - Scoped strictly to each repo directory. Writes code, tests, documentation, and verifies linting.\n5. **`coding-review`**:\n   - Collects all develop reports. Confirms cross-repo consistency and spec compliance.\n   - If defects found, loops back to `coding-plan`. If approved, asks user for merge confirmation.\n6. **`coding-merge`**:\n   - Automatically merges locally or opens a Pull Request with `gh` if remote exists.\n\n## Triggering the Workflow\n\n- Type `@coding <your feature or task description>`\n- Or prompt: \"Implement the following feature using the coding workflow: ...\"\n",
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/skills/coding-orchestrator/SKILL.md"
}

  "Content": "---\nname: coding-monitoring\ndescription: >-\n  Standalone worker subagent for background telemetry, application log surveillance,\n  and serial console monitoring with automatic error detection and pause gates.\n---\n\n# Coding Monitoring (`@monitoring`)\n\nThe `coding-monitoring` subagent streams and watches continuous output sources to intercept crashes, errors, or anomalies early.\n\n## Capabilities\n\n- **Log & Stream Surveillance**: Tails application logs, Docker stdout, or serial port streams (`/dev/tty*`).\n- **Error Detection**: Detects exceptions, stack traces, and custom alert conditions.\n- **Intervention Gate**: Pauses monitoring immediately when an anomaly is discovered, formulating a structured `MonitoringAlert` and asking the user via `ask_question` how to proceed.\n- **Multiple Concurrent Instances**: Run separate monitors simultaneously, differentiated by target identifiers (e.g., `coding-monitoring-serial`, `coding-monitoring-backend`).\n\n## Triggering Monitoring\n\n- Type `@monitoring watch serial port /dev/ttyUSB0 at 115200 baud`\n- Or prompt: \"Start monitoring server logs at /var/log/app.log and alert me on any 5xx errors.\"\n",

}

  "Content": "\"\"\"Tests for gp_coding WorkflowState management and concurrency locking.\"\"\"\n\nimport pytest\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom gp_coding.state import WorkflowState, WorkflowStatus, WorkflowConflictError\n\n\ndef test_initial_state_is_idle():\n    state = WorkflowState()\n    assert state.status == WorkflowStatus.IDLE\n    assert not state.status.is_active()\n    assert not state.status.is_waiting_user_input()\n\n\ndef test_start_workflow_success_and_conflict():\n    state = WorkflowState()\n    state.start_workflow(workflow_id=\"wf-1\", repositories=[\"repo-a\"])\n    assert state.status == WorkflowStatus.CLARIFYING\n    assert state.current_step == \"coding-clarify\"\n    assert state.status.is_active()\n\n    # Attempting to start a second workflow while wf-1 is active must raise WorkflowConflictError\n    with pytest.raises(WorkflowConflictError) as exc_info:\n        state.start_workflow(workflow_id=\"wf-2\")\n    assert \"another workflow 'wf-1' is currently active\" in str(exc_info.value)\n\n\ndef test_workflow_transitions_and_waiting():\n    state = WorkflowState()\n    state.start_workflow(\"wf-1\")\n\n    # Move to coding-specify awaiting approval\n    state.transition_to(\n        status=WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL,\n        step=\"coding-specify\",\n        waiting_agent=\"coding-specify\",\n        waiting_conversation_id=\"conv-spec-123\",\n    )\n    assert state.status.is_waiting_user_input()\n    assert state.waiting_agent == \"coding-specify\"\n    assert state.waiting_conversation_id == \"conv-spec-123\"\n\n    # Move to planning\n    state.transition_to(\n        status=WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_PLAN_APPROVAL,\n        step=\"coding-plan\",\n        waiting_agent=\"coding-plan\",\n        waiting_conversation_id=\"conv-plan-456\",\n    )\n    assert state.status.is_waiting_user_input()\n    assert state.waiting_agent == \"coding-plan\"\n\n    # Complete workflow\n    state.complete_workflow()\n    assert state.status == WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED\n    assert not state.status.is_active()\n    assert state.waiting_agent is None\n\n    # Now a new workflow can start cleanly\n    state.start_workflow(\"wf-2\")\n    assert state.workflow_id == \"wf-2\"\n    assert state.status == WorkflowStatus.CLARIFYING\n\n\ndef test_state_persistence_roundtrip(tmp_path: Path):\n    state_file = tmp_path / \"state.json\"\n    state = WorkflowState()\n    state.start_workflow(\"wf-persist\", repositories=[\"frontend\", \"backend\"])\n    state.transition_to(\n        status=WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL,\n        step=\"coding-specify\",\n        waiting_agent=\"coding-specify\",\n        waiting_conversation_id=\"conv-abc\",\n    )\n    state.save(path=state_file)\n\n    loaded = WorkflowState.load(path=state_file)\n    assert loaded.workflow_id == \"wf-persist\"\n    assert loaded.status == WorkflowStatus.AWAITING_SPEC_APPROVAL\n    assert loaded.waiting_agent == \"coding-specify\"\n    assert loaded.waiting_conversation_id == \"conv-abc\"\n    assert loaded.repositories == [\"frontend\", \"backend\"]\n",

return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"clarify\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=\"to_specify\"),\n    )\n\n\ndef specify_stage(node_input: Any) -> Event:\n    \"\"\"Stage 2: Specification synthesis and artifact generation (coding-specify).\"\"\"\n    data = node_input if isinstance(node_input, dict) else {}\n    approved = data.get(\"approved\")\n    if approved is True:\n        route = \"approved\"\n    elif approved is False:\n        route = \"rejected\"\n    else:\n        route = \"awaiting_approval\"\n\n    return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"specify\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=route),\n    )\n\n\ndef plan_stage(node_input: Any) -> Event:\n    \"\"\"Stage 3: Multi-repo architecture planning and branch naming (coding-plan).\"\"\"\n    data = node_input if isinstance(node_input, dict) else {}\n    approved = data.get(\"approved\")\n    if approved is True:\n        route = \"approved\"\n    elif approved is False:\n        route = \"rejected\"\n    else:\n        route = \"awaiting_approval\"\n\n    return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"plan\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=route),\n    )\n\n\ndef develop_stage(node_input: Any) -> Event:\n    \"\"\"Stage 4: Isolated per-repo development (coding-develop).\"\"\"\n    return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"develop\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=\"to_review\"),\n    )\n\n\ndef review_stage(node_input: Any) -> Event:\n    \"\"\"Stage 5: Cross-repo consistency and spec validation (coding-review).\"\"\"\n    data = node_input if isinstance(node_input, dict) else {}\n    has_discrepancies = bool(data.get(\"discrepancies\"))\n    route = \"discrepancy\" if has_discrepancies else \"approved\"\n\n    return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"review\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=route),\n    )\n\n\ndef merge_stage(node_input: Any) -> Event:\n    \"\"\"Stage 6: Branch merging or PR submission (coding-merge).\"\"\"\n    return Event(\n        output={\"stage\": \"merge\", \"result\": node_input},\n        actions=EventActions(route=\"completed\"),\n    )\n\n\ndef create_coding_orchestrator_workflow() -> Workflow:\n    \"\"\"Instantiate the ADK Workflow graph for coding orchestration.\"\"\"\n    edges = [\n        (START, clarify_stage),\n        (clarify_stage, specify_stage),\n        (specify_stage, {\"approved\": plan_stage, \"rejected\": clarify_stage}),\n        (plan_stage, {\"approved\": develop_stage, \"rejected\": clarify_stage}),\n        (develop_stage, review_stage),\n        (review_stage, {\"discrepancy\": plan_stage, \"approved\": merge_stage}),\n    ]\n\n    return Workflow(\n        name=\"coding_orchestrator\",\n        description=ORCHESTRATOR_DESCRIPTION,\n        edges=edges,\n    )\n",
  "Overwrite": true,
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/orchestrator.py"
}

assert ev_review_disc.actions.route == \"discrepancy\"\n\n    # Merge\n    ev_merge = merge_stage({\"merged\": True})\n    assert ev_merge.actions.route == \"completed\"\n",

It bundles an end-to-end multi-agent coding workflow orchestrated with **Google ADK 2.0 (`Workflow`)**, background worker subagents powered by **`google-antigravity`**, standalone telemetry monitoring, and a non-autoregressive **Laya Router** pre-invocation prompt triage hook.\n\n---\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Manifest & Setup (`plugin.json` & `scripts/post_install.sh`)\n- Manifest: [`plugin.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/plugin.json) declaring name `gp-coding-plugin`, version `0.1.0`, and `post_install` script.\n- Package spec: [`pyproject.toml`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/pyproject.toml) declaring dependencies: `google-antigravity`, `google-adk`, `laya`, `pydantic`, `pytest`.\n- Environment Provisioning: [`scripts/post_install.sh`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/scripts/post_install.sh) runs `uv sync` to build the isolated `.venv`.\n\n### 2. Pre-Invocation Prompt Triage Hook (`hooks.json` & `hooks/prompt_triage.py`)\n- Registered via [`hooks.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks.json) on `PreInvocation`.\n- Powered by **Laya Router** executing a 3-stage decision pipeline:\n  1. **Session Scope Guard**: Assesses whether prompt is within technical session scope (threshold $\\ge 0.80$; bypassed on `invocationNum <= 1`). Injects guidance to prompt user if out-of-scope.\n  2. **Waiting Subagent Route**: Checks `WorkflowState` for background subagents awaiting user input (e.g. `coding-specify` awaiting spec approval, `coding-plan` awaiting plan approval, `coding-review` awaiting merge approval). If matched ($\\ge 0.80$), directs input directly to that subagent via `send_message`.\n  3. **Intent Triage**:\n     - *Question*: Injects `\"Prompt Triage Directive: Do not make any changes.\"`\n     - *Coding*: Injects instruction to invoke `@coding` (`coding-orchestrator`), or notes active workflow status if one is already running.\n     - *Monitoring*: Injects instruction to invoke `@monitoring` (`coding-monitoring`).\n\n### 3. Coding Orchestrator ADK Workflow (`@coding`)\n- File: [`src/gp_coding/agents/orchestrator.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/orchestrator.py)\n- Implemented as an ADK 2.0 `Workflow` graph executing:\n  ```text\n  coding-clarify ──> coding-specify ──> coding-plan ──> coding-develop (parallel per repo)\n                            ▲               ▲                      │\n                            │ (reject/fb)   │ (reject/fb)          ▼\n                            └───────────────┴────────────── coding-review\n                                                                   │ (approved)\n                                                                   ▼\n                                                             coding-merge\n  ```\n- Enforces single active coding workflow policy via [`src/gp_coding/state.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/state.py).\n\n### 4. Worker Subagents (`src/gp_coding/agents/`)\n- **`coding-clarify`**: Interrogates requirements using Matt Pocock `/grill-me` protocol via `ask_question`. Model: `gemini-3.8-flash`.\n- **`coding-specify`**: Consolidates clarifications into `specification.md` (Artifact) with feedback loops. Model: `gemini-3.8-flash`.\n- **`coding-plan`**: Multi-repo architecture planner and branch strategist (`feature/*`, `task/*`, `bugfix/*`), emitting `plan.md` (Artifact). Model: `gemini-3.8-pro`.\n- **`coding-develop`**: Isolated per-repo development worker (`coding-develop-<repo>`) writing code, unit tests, linting, docs, and CI/CD. Model: `gemini-3.8-pro`.\n- **`coding-review`**: Cross-repo verification and spec compliance validator; triggers revised plans on discrepancies, gates on user merge approval. Model: `gemini-3.8-pro`.\n- **`coding-merge`**: Git branch merge or Pull Request submission via GitHub CLI. Model: `gemini-3.8-flash-lite`.\n- **`coding-monitoring`** (`@monitoring`): Standalone background stream and log watcher with anomaly pause gates. Model: `gemini-3.8-flash-lite`.\n\n### 5. Structured Schemas & Converters (`src/gp_coding/schemas.py`)\n- Pydantic models for rigid JSON outputs across all agents.\n- Dedicated Markdown converters formatting structured data into user-facing presentation tables, callouts, and artifacts.\n\n### 6. Skills (`skills/`)\n- `clean-code-reviewer`: Automated clean code review and refactoring assistant.\n- `coding-orchestrator`: Guide for the 6-phase coding orchestrator workflow (`@coding`).\n- `coding-monitoring`: Guide for background telemetry and log surveillance (`@monitoring`).\n\n### 7. Rules (`rules/AGENTS.md`)\n- Ingested by Antigravity to inform the main agent of all subagents, mentions, background waiting protocols, and routing directives.\n",

### 🤖 Agent (Step 273 - 2026-10-25T17:35:21Z)

The implementation of the subagents, ADK coding orchestrator workflow, and Laya Router prompt triage hooks for [`gp-coding-plugin`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin) is complete.

### Summary of Completed Work

1. **Pre-Invocation Prompt Triage Hook**:
   - Registered in [`hooks.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks.json) executing [`hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py).
   - Driven by **Laya Router** ([`src/gp_coding/triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/triage.py)) with a 3-stage pipeline:
     1. **Session Scope Guard** ($\ge 0.80$, bypassed on session start).
     2. **Waiting Subagent Routing** ($\ge 0.80$, directly routes user feedback to awaiting background subagents via `send_message`).
     3. **Intent Triage** ($\ge 0.80$ auto-routing or user confirmation): questions receive *"Do not make any changes"*, coding requests route to `@coding`, and monitoring requests route to `@monitoring`.

2. **Coding Orchestrator ADK Workflow (`@coding`)**:
   - Implemented in [`src/gp_coding/agents/orchestrator.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/orchestrator.py) using **Google ADK 2.0 (`Workflow`)**.
   - Enforces the 6-stage lifecycle: `coding-clarify` $\rightarrow$ `coding-specify` $\rightarrow$ `coding-plan` $\rightarrow$ `coding-develop` $\rightarrow$ `coding-review` $\rightarrow$ `coding-merge`.
   - Concurrency lock in [`src/gp_coding/state.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/state.py) guarantees only one coding workflow runs at a time.

3. **Antigravity Worker Subagents (`src/gp_coding/agents/`)**:
   - Implemented with `google-antigravity` (`LocalAgentConfig` + `Agent`) and matched complexity models:
     - [`clarify.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/clarify.py) (`coding-clarify`): Matt Pocock `/grill-me` interviewer (`gemini-3.8-flash`).
     - [`specify.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/specify.py) (`coding-specify`): `specification.md` artifact generator with approval/rejection loop (`gemini-3.8-flash`).
     - [`plan.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/plan.py) (`coding-plan`): Multi-repo architect & branch strategist emitting `plan.md` (`gemini-3.8-pro`).
     - [`develop.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/develop.py) (`coding-develop-<repo>`): Isolated per-repo developer (`gemini-3.8-pro`).
     - [`review.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/review.py) (`coding-review`): Cross-repo consistency and spec validation gatekeeper (`gemini-3.8-pro`).
     - [`merge.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/merge.py) (`coding-merge`): Local git merge or GitHub Pull Request manager (`gemini-3.8-flash-lite`).
     - [`monitoring.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/agents/monitoring.py) (`@monitoring`): Telemetry & stream surveillance with pause gates (`gemini-3.8-flash-lite`).

4. **Rigid Schemas & Markdown Converters**:
   - Pydantic models in [`src/gp_coding/schemas.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/schemas.py) and renderers in [`src/gp_coding/utils/markdown.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/utils/markdown.py).

5. **Rules, Skills & Documentation**:
   - Updated [`rules/AGENTS.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/rules/AGENTS.md) and [`plugin.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/plugin.json).
   - Added skills [`skills/coding-orchestrator/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/skills/coding-orchestrator/SKILL.md) and [`skills/coding-monitoring/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/skills/coding-monitoring/SKILL.md).
   - Documented in Obsidian Vault at [`obsidian-vault/plugins/gp-coding-plugin.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/obsidian-vault/plugins/gp-coding-plugin.md).
   - Session transcript archived to [`obsidian-vault/sessions/8dbcd1fd-91d2-4910-b07a-5089b2b16bc9.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/obsidian-vault/sessions/8dbcd1fd-91d2-4910-b07a-5089b2b16bc9.md).

6. **Verification**:

{
  "CommandLine": "gp-coding-plugin/.venv/bin/python -c \"\nimport asyncio\nfrom google.antigravity import types\nfrom google.antigravity.hooks import HookContext, PreTurnHook, pre_turn\nfrom gp_coding.triage import LayaTriageEngine\n\nclass PromptTriageHook(PreTurnHook):\n    def __init__(self, use_router: bool = False):\n        self.engine = LayaTriageEngine(use_router=use_router)\n\n    async def run(self, context: HookContext, data: types.Content) -> types.HookResult:\n        prompt_text = data if isinstance(data, str) else str(data)\n        inv_num = context.get_state('invocation_num', 1)\n        res = self.engine.triage(prompt=prompt_text, invocation_num=inv_num)\n        context.set_state('triage_result', res)\n        if not res.in_scope and res.scope_confidence >= 0.8:\n            return types.HookResult(allow=False, message='Out of scope')\n        return types.HookResult(allow=True, message=res.intent or '')\n\nctx = HookContext()\nhook = PromptTriageHook(use_router=False)\nres = asyncio.run(hook.run(ctx, 'Can you explain this file?'))\nprint('Result allow:', res.allow, 'message:', res.message)\ntriage_res = ctx.get_state('triage_result')\nprint('Triage intent:', triage_res.intent)\n\"",

{
  "Content": "\"\"\"Google Antigravity SDK style hooks for prompt triage and session lifecycle.\n\nImplements PreTurnHook and decorators using google.antigravity.hooks.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport warnings\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\n\nos.environ[\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_PROGRESS_BARS\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM\"] = \"false\"\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\")\n\nfrom google.antigravity import types\nfrom google.antigravity.hooks import (\n    HookContext,\n    PreTurnHook,\n    OnSessionStartHook,\n    pre_turn,\n    on_session_start,\n)\n\nfrom gp_coding.state import WorkflowState\nfrom gp_coding.triage import LayaTriageEngine, TriageResult\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef extract_prompt_text(data: types.Content) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Extract plain text string from SDK types.Content primitive or list.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(data, str):\n        return data\n    if isinstance(data, list):\n        parts: list[str] = []\n        for item in data:\n            if isinstance(item, str):\n                parts.append(item)\n            elif hasattr(item, \"text\") and getattr(item, \"text\"):\n                parts.append(str(getattr(item, \"text\")))\n            elif hasattr(item, \"name\") and getattr(item, \"name\"):\n                parts.append(f\"/{getattr(item, 'name')}\")\n        return \" \".join(parts)\n    if hasattr(data, \"text\") and getattr(data, \"text\"):\n        return str(getattr(data, \"text\"))\n    return str(data)\n\n\nclass PromptTriageHook(PreTurnHook):\n    \"\"\"Google Antigravity PreTurnHook that triages user prompts via Laya Router.\n\n    Executes the 3-stage triage pipeline:\n    1. Scope check (gates out-of-scope prompts)\n    2. Waiting subagent routing (forwards input to waiting worker agents)\n    3. Intent classification (Question with no-change directive, Coding, Monitoring)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, use_router: bool = True):\n        self.engine = LayaTriageEngine(use_router=use_router)\n\n    async def run(self, context: HookContext, data: types.Content) -> types.HookResult:\n        prompt_text = extract_prompt_text(data).strip()\n        if not prompt_text:\n            return types.HookResult(allow=True, message=\"empty prompt\")\n\n        inv_num = context.get_state(\"invocation_num\", 1)\n        conv_id = context.get_state(\"conversation_id\", None)\n        artifact_dir = context.get_state(\"artifact_dir\", None)\n\n        state = WorkflowState.load(artifact_dir=artifact_dir, conversation_id=conv_id)\n        result: TriageResult = self.engine.triage(\n            prompt=prompt_text,\n            invocation_num=inv_num,\n            state=state,\n        )\n\n        # Store triage telemetry in HookContext\n        context.set_state(\"triage_result\", result)\n        context.set_state(\"inject_steps\", result.inject_steps)\n        context.set_state(\"triage_intent\", result.intent)\n        context.set_state(\"routed_to_waiting_agent\", result.routed_to_waiting_agent)\n\n        # If prompt is determined out of scope with high confidence, deny turn with guidance\n        if not result.in_scope and result.scope_confidence >= 0.80:\n            return types.HookResult(\n                allow=False,\n                message=(\n                    \"Prompt appears out of the scope of this session. \"\n                    \"Please ask the user whether they want to broaden the scope or stop.\"\n                ),\n            )\n\n        return types.HookResult(\n            allow=True,\n            message=result.intent or \"allowed\",\n        )\n\n\n@pre_turn\nasync def prompt_triage_pre_turn(context: HookContext, data: types.Content) -> types.HookResult:\n    \"\"\"Decorator-based Antigravity PreTurnHook for prompt triage.\"\"\"\n    hook = PromptTriageHook(use_router=True)\n    return await hook.run(context, data)\n\n\n@on_session_start\nasync def session_start_triage(context: HookContext) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Antigravity OnSessionStartHook initializing triage state.\"\"\"\n    context.set_state(\"triage_initialized\", True)\n    context.set_state(\"invocation_num\", 1)\n",

\"extract_prompt_text\",\n]\n\n\nasync def run_hook_async(payload: dict) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Execute PromptTriageHook within an Antigravity HookContext.\"\"\"\n    prompt = payload.get(\"lastUserInput\", \"\")\n    if not prompt:\n        return []\n\n    context = HookContext()\n    context.set_state(\"invocation_num\", payload.get(\"invocationNum\", 1))\n    context.set_state(\"conversation_id\", payload.get(\"conversationId\"))\n    context.set_state(\"artifact_dir\", payload.get(\"artifactDirectoryPath\"))\n\n    hook = PromptTriageHook(use_router=True)\n    await hook.run(context, prompt)\n\n    inject_steps = context.get_state(\"inject_steps\", [])\n    return inject_steps if isinstance(inject_steps, list) else = json.loads(raw_input)\n        inject_steps = asyncio.run(run_hook_async(payload))\n        json.dump({\"injectSteps\": inject_steps}, sys.stdout)\n    except Exception:\n        # Hooks must never ### 🤖 Agent (Step 354 - 2026-10-25T17:49:03Z)

[`gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py) has been refactored to use native **`google-antigravity`** style hooks:

### Key Changes Made:
1. **Antigravity Hook Classes & Decorators** ([`src/gp_coding/hooks.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/src/gp_coding/hooks.py)):
   - Implemented `PromptTriageHook(PreTurnHook)` inheriting from `google.antigravity.hooks.PreTurnHook`.
   - Method signature:
     ```python
     async def run(self, context: HookContext, data: types.Content) -> types.HookResult:
     ```
   - Employs `HookContext` (`context.set_state(...)`, `context.get_state(...)`) for lifecycle state management.
   - Returns typed `google.antigravity.types.HookResult(allow=True/False, message=...)`.
   - Also exports the `@pre_turn` decorator `prompt_triage_pre_turn` and `@on_session_start` decorator `session_start_triage`.

2. **Dual Execution Support in [`hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py)**:
   - **Python SDK Mode**: Importable directly as `PromptTriageHook` or `prompt_triage_pre_turn` for `LocalAgentConfig(hooks=[...])`.
   - **CLI Mode**: Executes `run_hook_async(payload)` within an asynchronous `HookContext`, reading `stdin` JSON and emitting `{"injectSteps": [...]}` to `stdout` for runtime `hooks.json` lifecycle execution.

3. **Verification**:
   - Expanded test suite in [`tests/test_prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/tests/test_prompt_triage.py) verifying `PromptTriageHook` run logic, `HookContext` telemetry recording, `session_start_triage`, and CLI subprocess invocation.
   - All **37/37 tests pass** cleanly via `pytest`.

### 👤 User (Step 355 - 2026-10-26T08:32:17Z)

/plan we are going to take a different approach for the agents

- instead of coding them in python, we are going to generate .agents/agents/<agent_name>.md
- the workflow will be translated to instructions, asking to use invoke_subagent when the steps move to the next part
- the @[gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py]  will be registered as PreInvocation hook, it will alter the prompt to instruct the main agent to handle the result of the triage
- the gp-coding-plugin/hooks/register_subagents.py will be registered as SessionStart hook and will define the @coding and @monitoring subagents

> The approach shifts to defining agents as Markdown files instead of Python code. Workflows will be expressed as instructions within these Markdown files, leveraging a subagent invocation for sequential steps. A pre-invocation hook will be registered to triage prompts and guide the main agent's handling of the results.
> 
> 
> The shift involves representing subagents as Markdown files containing instructions, descriptions, and transition triggers, replacing Python code. A session start hook will register the main `@coding` and `@monitoring` subagents, implementing an instruction-driven workflow. The workflow is orchestrated by `coding-orchestrator`, which uses `invoke_subagent` to step through tasks such as clarification and specification.
> 
> 
> The core workflow comprises sequential agent invocations for clarification, specification, planning, development, review, and merging, handling artifacts at each stage. A pre-invocation hook triages prompts using a router to assess session scope and waiting subagents. This hook directs prompt routing, including resuming agents awaiting user input via message alterations.
> 
> 
> A prompt triage system is being implemented to handle different request types. Questions will prevent modifications, coding requests will initiate the orchestrator, and monitoring requests will activate the monitoring subagent. This triage will be handled via a pre-invocation hook.
> 
> The `SessionStart` hook registers all subagents, creating necessary Markdown files and injecting session-level instructions for the main agent. This hook defines the mappings between agent identifiers (e.g., `@coding`) and the actual subagent orchestrator. The hook's main function sets up subagent definitions.

  "Content": "# Implementation Plan: Markdown-Defined Subagents & SessionStart Registration Hook\n\nThis plan details the architectural pivot for [`gp-coding-plugin`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin):\n1. **Markdown Agent Declarations**: Shift subagent definitions from compiled Python classes to declarative markdown specification files under `.agents/agents/<agent_name>.md`.\n2. **Instruction-Driven Orchestration**: Replace hardcoded Python graph workflows with prompt instructions directing orchestrators and workers to transition phases using `invoke_subagent`.\n3. **PreInvocation Prompt Triage Hook**: Use [`hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py) (Laya Router) to rewrite prompts and instruct the main agent on how to handle the triage output.\n4. **SessionStart Registration Hook**: Implement [`hooks/register_subagents.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/register_subagents.py) as a `SessionStart` hook to initialize `.agents/agents/` and register `@coding` and `@monitoring` subagents into the session context.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Goal Description\n\nTransition `gp-coding-plugin` subagent architecture:\n- Define worker and orchestrator subagents as modular markdown files in `.agents/agents/<agent_name>.md`.\n- Enable the main agent and subagents to drive the 6-phase coding lifecycle (`coding-clarify` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-specify` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-plan` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-develop` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-review` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-merge`) via `invoke_subagent` and `send_message`.\n- Register [`hooks/register_subagents.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/register_subagents.py) under `SessionStart` in `hooks.json` to configure subagents, sync definitions, and inject guidance mapping `@coding` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-orchestrator` and `@monitoring` $\\rightarrow$ `coding-monitoring`.\n- Retain Laya Router triage in [`hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py) (`PreInvocation`), updating injected prompt alterations to direct the main agent to invoke `@coding` / `@monitoring` via `invoke_subagent` or forward messages to awaiting subagents via `send_message`.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Architecture & Lifecycle Overview\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph SessionStartPhase [\"1. {
  "Content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"SessionStart Hook: Registers subagents and injects orchestration guidance.\n\nReads agent specifications from gp-coding-plugin/agents/*.md and injects\nsession startup instructions declaring @coding, @monitoring, and all worker subagents.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nPLUGIN_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nAGENTS_DIR = PLUGIN_ROOT / \"agents\"\n\n\ndef parse_agent_frontmatter(md_path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract YAML frontmatter name and description from a markdown file.\"\"\"\n    content = md_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    fm_match = re.match(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n---\", content, re.DOTALL)\n    metadata = {}\n    if fm_match:\n        for line in fm_match.group(1).splitlines():\n            if \":\" in line:\n                key, val = line.split(\":\", 1)\n                metadata[key.strip()] = val.strip().strip('\"').strip(\"'\")\n    if \"name\" not in metadata:\n        metadata[\"name\"] = md_path.stem\n    return metadata\n\n\ndef discover_agents() -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Scan gp-coding-plugin/agents/ for all markdown agent definitions.\"\"\"\n    agents = []\n    if not AGENTS_DIR.exists():\n        return agents\n    for md_file in sorted(AGENTS_DIR.glob(\"*.md\")):\n        meta = parse_agent_frontmatter(md_file)\n        meta[\"file_path\"] = str(md_file)\n        agents.append(meta)\n    return agents\n\n\ndef build_session_guidance(agents: list[dict[str, str]]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Construct system guidance describing available subagents and orchestration protocol.\"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        \"gp-coding-plugin Subagent System Initialized:\",\n        \"\",\n        \"Available Subagents (defined in gp-coding-plugin/agents/):\",\n    ]\n    for agent in agents:\n        name = agent.get(\"name\", \"\")\n        desc = agent.get(\"description\", \"\")\n        lines.append(f\"- `@{name}` ({name}): {desc}\")\n\n    lines.extend([\n        \"\",\n        \"Subagent
