{
  "Content": "# Coding Standards & Subagent Coordination — gp-coding-plugin\n\nThis rule set applies whenever `gp-coding-plugin` is active in an Antigravity session.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Core Engineering Principles\n- **Clarity over Cleverness**: Write readable, expressive code with self-explanatory naming and explicit typing.\n- **Fail Fast & Explicitly**: Validate boundaries, function arguments, and I/O operations early.\n- **Single Responsibility**: Functions and classes should have one well-defined responsibility.\n- **Preserve Comments & Docstrings**: Never strip existing comments or docstrings unless explicitly instructed.\n- **Clickable Links**: Always create clickable file and code symbol links using markdown with the `file://` scheme.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Subagent Architecture & Mentions\n\nSubagents in `gp-coding-plugin` are declaratively authored as markdown files located in [`agents/`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/). When users mention these subagents (e.g. `@coding`, `@monitoring`), or when the prompt triage hook directs you to invoke them, you must use `invoke_subagent` and `send_message`.\n\n### 2.1 Mentionable Subagents & Declarations\n\n| Mention Handle | Agent Name | Declaration File | Role |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| **`@coding`** | `coding-orchestrator` | [`agents/coding-orchestrator.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-orchestrator.md) | Coordinates the 6-stage development lifecycle using `invoke_subagent` |\n| **`@monitoring`** | `coding-monitoring` | [`agents/coding-monitoring.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-monitoring.md) | Telemetry & stream surveillance (logs, serial console) |\n| `@coding-clarify` | `coding-clarify` | [`agents/coding-clarify.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-clarify.md) | Matt Pocock `/grill-me` requirement questionnaire |\n| `@coding-specify` | `coding-specify` | [`agents/coding-specify.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-specify.md) | Requirement synthesis & `specification.md` artifact |\n| `@coding-plan` | `coding-plan` | [`agents/coding-plan.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-plan.md) | Multi-repo architecture & `plan.md` artifact |\n| `@coding-develop-<repo>`| `coding-develop` | [`agents/coding-develop.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-develop.md) | Per-repo isolated development & test execution |\n| `@coding-review` | `coding-review` | [`agents/coding-review.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-review.md) | Cross-repo consistency & specification validation |\n| `@coding-merge` | `coding-merge` | [`agents/coding-merge.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-merge.md) | Git branch merge / Pull Request submission |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Instruction-Driven Workflow Lifecycle\n\nThe workflow transitions through phases via instructions, calling `invoke_subagent` at each step:\n\n```text\ncoding-clarify ──> coding-specify ──> coding-plan ──> coding-develop (parallel per repo)\n                          ▲               ▲                      │\n                          │ (reject/fb)   │ (reject/fb)          ▼\n                          └───────────────┴────────────── coding-review\n                                                                 │ (approved)\n                                                                 ▼\n                                                           coding-merge\n```\n\n### 3.1 Lifecycle Rules:\n1. **Single Workflow Concurrency**: Only ONE coding workflow can run at a time. The workflow state is tracked in `workflow_state.json`. Before starting a new workflow, ensure the active one is either `COMPLETED` or `FAILED`.\n2. **Instruction-Driven Transitions**: The orchestrator (`coding-orchestrator`) and worker agents execute phase handoffs by calling `invoke_subagent` with the target `TypeName` and specific instructions.\n3. **Background Awaiting & Resumption**: When subagents await user decisions (`coding-specify` awaiting spec approval, `coding-plan` awaiting plan approval, `coding-review` awaiting merge approval), forward user feedback to the subagent using `send_message`.\n4. **Structured JSON Output**: Subagents return rigid structured JSON schemas defined in `gp_coding.schemas`. Always use the markdown presentation helpers to display results cleanly to the user.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Lifecycle Hooks\n\n1. **`SessionStart` (`hooks/register_subagents.py`)**:\n   - Reads `agents/*.md` definitions.\n   - Injects startup system instructions declaring `@coding`, `@monitoring`, and available worker subagents.\n2. **`PreInvocation` (`hooks/prompt_triage.py`)**:\n   - Uses **Laya Router** to evaluate incoming prompts.\n   - Session Scope Guard: Flags out-of-scope prompts with confidence $< 0.80$.\n   - Waiting Subagent Routing: Directs input to awaiting subagents via `send_message`.\n   - Intent Classification: Alters prompts to enforce read-only (\"Do not make any changes\") for questions, or prompts invocation of `@coding` / `@monitoring` via `invoke_subagent`.\n",

  "Content": "---\nplugin_name: \"gp-coding-plugin\"\nstatus: \"active\"\ntags:\n  - antigravity-plugin\n  - component-spec\n  - subagents\n  - markdown-agents\n  - laya-router\nversion: \"0.1.0\"\n---\n\n# Plugin: gp-coding-plugin\n\n## Overview\n`gp-coding-plugin` is the primary coding companion plugin within the `agentic` ecosystem. It bundles declarative markdown-defined subagents under [`gp-coding-plugin/agents/`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/), an instruction-driven 6-stage development lifecycle executed via `invoke_subagent`, a `SessionStart` subagent registration hook, and a non-autoregressive `PreInvocation` **Laya Router** prompt triage hook.\n\n---\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Manifest & Setup (`plugin.json` & `hooks.json`)\n- Manifest: [`plugin.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/plugin.json) declaring name `gp-coding-plugin`, version `0.1.0`, and `post_install` script.\n- Lifecycle Hooks: [`hooks.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks.json):\n  - **`SessionStart`**: Runs [`hooks/register_subagents.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/register_subagents.py) to discover agent definitions and register `@coding` and `@monitoring` into the session.\n  - **`PreInvocation`**: Runs [`hooks/prompt_triage.py`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/hooks/prompt_triage.py) using Laya Router for 3-stage prompt triage and alteration.\n\n### 2. Declarative Markdown Subagents (`agents/`)\nSubagent instructions are authored as modular markdown specifications with YAML frontmatter:\n- [`coding-orchestrator.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-orchestrator.md) (`@coding-orchestrator`): Coordinates the 6 phases using `invoke_subagent`.\n- [`coding.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding.md) (`@coding`): Short alias routing to `coding-orchestrator`.\n- [`coding-clarify.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-clarify.md): Matt Pocock `/grill-me` requirement questionnaire.\n- [`coding-specify.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-specify.md): Consolidates requirements into `specification.md` (Artifact).\n- [`coding-plan.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-plan.md): Deconstructs spec into multi-repo tasks and branch naming (`plan.md`).\n- [`coding-develop.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-develop.md): Scoped single-repo implementation, tests, linting, docs, and CI/CD.\n- [`coding-review.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-review.md): Cross-repo verification and discrepancy routing.\n- [`coding-merge.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-merge.md): Release engineer performing git merge or PR creation.\n- [`coding-monitoring.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/coding-monitoring.md): Continuous log / serial stream surveillance with error pause gates.\n- [`monitoring.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/gp-coding-plugin/agents/monitoring.md) (`@monitoring`): Short alias routing to `coding-monitoring`.\n\n### 3. Instruction-Driven Lifecycle Pipeline\nPhase transitions are guided by prompt instructions directing agents to use `invoke_subagent`:\n```text\ncoding-clarify ──> coding-specify ──> coding-plan ──> coding-develop (parallel per repo)\n                          ▲               ▲                      │\n                          │ (reject/fb)   │ (reject/fb)          ▼\n                          └───────────────┴────────────── coding-review\n                                                                 │ (approved)\n                                                                 ▼\n                                                           coding-merge\n```\n\n### 4. Laya Router Prompt Triage (`hooks/prompt_triage.py`)\n1. **Session Scope Guard** ($\\ge 0.80$): Out-of-scope prompts prompt the user to confirm broadening or stopping.\n2. **Waiting Subagent Route** ($\\ge 0.80$): Directs user responses to awaiting subagents via `send_message`.\n3. **Intent Classification**:\n   - *Question*: Injects `\"Prompt Triage Directive: Do not make any changes.\"`\n   - *Coding*: Injects instruction to invoke `@coding` (`coding-orchestrator`) using `invoke_subagent`.\n   - *Monitoring*: Injects instruction to invoke `@monitoring` (`coding-monitoring`) using `invoke_subagent`.\n",
